This will allow Wizz Air to easily use all available data in the organization to improve the sales pipeline, boost productivity, and improve customer satisfaction by delivering personalized and seamless engagement when prospects and customers are most receptive through their preferred channels.

Measurement and Optimization: We provide robust analytics and reporting tools that allow Wizz Air to measure the effectiveness of their personalized marketing campaigns in real time. By analyzing key metrics such as open rates, click-through rates, and conversion rates, they can continuously optimize their messaging strategies to deliver better results.
Overall, Dengage empowers Wizz Air to deliver personalized omnichannel customer interactions and experiences that drive loyalty, satisfaction, and revenue growth.

### Wizz Airâ€™s Non-Functional Requirements for Customer Engagement Platform

Goal #4: Providers shall specify in their proposal how their systems are compliant with GDPR and other applicable data protection regulations and relevant Wizz Air requirements.
Dengage Solution Capabilities: At Dengage, we prioritize data privacy and security to ensure that our platform aligns with regulatory requirements and meets the specific needs of our clients, including Wizz Air.
Here's how our platform addresses various rights under GDPR and other data protection regulations, as well as Privacy by Design principles:
GDPR Compliance: Dengage is committed to compliance with the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) and other global privacy laws. Our platform is designed to facilitate compliance with various rights granted to individuals under GDPR, including:
Right to Erasure: Dengage enables clients to promptly erase personal data upon request, ensuring compliance with individuals' right to have their data deleted.
Right to Access: Our platform provides mechanisms for individuals to access the personal data stored by our clients, allowing them to review and verify the accuracy of their information.
Right to Rectification: Dengage allows clients to easily update and rectify inaccurate or incomplete personal data, ensuring individuals' right to have their information corrected.
Right to Data Portability: We support data portability by facilitating the transfer of personal data to individuals or third parties in a commonly used and machine-readable format, as required by GDPR.
Right to Restriction of Processing: Our platform includes features to restrict the processing of personal data in certain circumstances, in compliance with individuals' right to restrict processing.
Right to Object: Dengage enables individuals to object to the processing of their data for specific purposes, as provided for by GDPR.
Right to Be Informed: We ensure transparency by providing clear and concise information to individuals about how their data is processed, including purposes, legal basis, and rights available to them.
Opt-Out of Campaign Messages: Our platform offers opt-out mechanisms for individuals to unsubscribe from campaign messages, ensuring compliance with regulations governing direct marketing communications.



Privacy by Design: Dengage incorporates Privacy by Design principles into our platform's architecture and development processes. This includes:
Data Minimization: We encourage clients to collect and process only the minimum amount of personal data necessary for their engagement purposes, reducing the risk of privacy breaches.
Security by Default: Our platform implements robust security measures by default, including encryption, access controls, and authentication mechanisms, to protect personal data against unauthorized access or disclosure.
User Consent Management: Dengage provides tools for managing user consent preferences and obtaining explicit consent where required by law, ensuring lawful and transparent data processing.
Data Lifecycle Management: We assist clients in implementing data retention policies and automated deletion processes to ensure that personal data is retained only for as long as necessary and securely disposed of when no longer needed.
We are fully committed to supporting Wizz Air's requirements for data protection and compliance and would be happy to provide further details or address any specific questions you may have in this regard. Refer Annex 2 to more details for Dengageâ€™s GDPR Compliance
Goal #5: Bidders are required to present an AOC document, to confirm PCI-DSS compliancy on the full payment and reservation system
Dengage Solution Capabilities: We appreciate the opportunity to clarify that Dengageâ€™s Customer Engagement Platform, while comprehensive in its capabilities for enhancing customer interactions and experiences, does not encompass payment processing or reservation systems. While PCI DSS compliance is not relevant to the Dengage Customer Engagement Platform, we prioritize data security and adhere to industry best practices to safeguard customer information within our platform. Our commitment to security includes robust encryption, access controls, and regular security assessments to protect sensitive data and ensure compliance with relevant data protection regulations.

Goal #6: Providers shall specify what the capabilities of their systems are to archive data
Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage's platform offers robust capabilities for archiving data, ensuring that valuable customer information is securely stored and easily accessible when needed. Here are some key features of our archiving methodology:

- Flexible Data Retention Policies: Dengage allows clients to define custom data retention policies (Default is 12 months) based on their specific requirements. This enables you to set rules for how long different types of data are retained within the platform before being archived or deleted.

- Secure Storage: Dengage employs industry-standard security measures to ensure the integrity and confidentiality of archived data. Our platform utilizes encrypted storage and access controls to protect sensitive information from unauthorized access or tampering.

- Easy Retrieval: Archived data can be easily retrieved through our platform's Remote Source Functionality or APIs. This allows you to access historical information for analysis, reporting, or compliance purposes without difficulty.

- Compliance with Data Regulations: Our archiving capabilities are designed to help clients comply with data protection regulations and industry standards. Whether it's GDPR or other regulatory requirements, Dengage provides tools and features to support your compliance efforts.

- Scalability: Dengage's archiving system is built to scale with your business needs, accommodating large volumes of data efficiently and effectively.

We understand that data archiving is a critical aspect of data management and are committed to providing robust and reliable solutions to meet your needs

Goal #9: The provider shall be able to demonstrate the capability to ensure timely message delivery, utilizing a throttle delivery mechanism. Throttle delivery should allow for the controlled release of email and push messages to optimize delivery rates and prevent risks associated with high-volume sending. The proposed solution should cover how this mechanism is implemented, tracked, and adjusted to meet the business needs and requirements regarding delivery
Dengage Solution Capabilities: We understand the importance of ensuring optimized delivery rates while mitigating risks associated with high-volume sending. Dengageâ€™s platform is equipped with advanced features to meet these needs effectively.

The throttle delivery mechanism within the Dengage platform is designed to allow for the controlled release of email, push, SMS, WhatsApp, In-App, and other messages. This mechanism operates by dynamically adjusting the message delivery rate based on various factors such as server load, recipient engagement patterns, and feedback loops from ISPs (Internet Service Providers). By monitoring these factors in real-time, Dengage ensures that messages are sent out at an optimal pace to maximize deliverability and prevent issues like inbox congestion or spam filtering. Key aspects of our throttle delivery mechanism include:

- Dynamic Rate Adjustment: Dengage continuously monitors delivery metrics and adjusts the sending rate dynamically to maintain optimal deliverability. This includes throttling or increasing message delivery rates as needed to adapt to changing conditions.

- Recipient Engagement Tracking: Our platform tracks recipient engagement metrics such as open rates, click-through rates, and bounce rates to gauge user responsiveness. This data is used to adjust delivery rates and prioritize sending to highly engaged users, ensuring maximum impact.

- ISP Feedback Integration: Dengage integrates with ISP feedback loops to monitor delivery performance and identify any issues such as spam complaints or delivery failures promptly. This feedback is used to refine the throttle delivery strategy and maintain a positive sender reputation.

- Customizable Settings: The throttle delivery mechanism in Dengage is fully customizable, allowing users to set specific thresholds and parameters based on their business needs and requirements. This includes adjusting delivery rates, defining priority segments, and configuring automated responses to delivery issues.

- Real-time Monitoring and Reporting: Dengage provides real-time monitoring and reporting capabilities, allowing users to track delivery performance, adjust settings on the fly, and generate actionable insights to optimize future campaigns.

In summary, Dengage's throttle delivery mechanism offers a comprehensive solution for ensuring timely message delivery while maintaining optimal deliverability rates. Our platform is equipped with advanced features and customizable settings to meet the unique needs and requirements of Wizz Air, ensuring maximum effectiveness and impact for your marketing campaigns.

## SOLUTION OVERVIEW


### The Dengage Customer-Driven Marketing Platform (CDMP)


The Dengage platform is a full end-to-end solution that requires no external technologies to function. The technology contains everything Wizz Air will need to deliver exceptional customer experiences through all key channels. Following is a list of the core components which make up the Dengage Customer Driven Marketing Platform:



### Data Unification â€“ Customer Data Platform

With a Customer Data Platform, at its core Dengage allows you to collect, unify, and activate complete profiles of every known and anonymous customer. Dengage enables integration with virtually any data sources through robust web services, pre-built integrations, and a simple user interface for file uploads. This will allow your team to use actionable data stored in customer profiles, account history, click-through behavior, and other information when segmenting known and anonymous contacts, using dynamic content, and personalizing campaigns.

#### Data Management

One of the hallmarks of the Dengage platform is the Data Model that will enable Wizz Air to utilize any data, from any data source, in its standard format to drive more targeted, relevant, personalized campaigns. Built on an open relational data model, the Dengage Customer Data Platform will enable you to use data without spending the effort (and cost) to make it fit into a pre-set structure that so many other platforms force you into. This flexibility, enabled by the data model, will allow you to speak to customers as individuals, respond to events and behaviors as they happen, and avoid the reliance on IT projects to execute your campaigns and manage your sales pipeline.
Open and extensible data structure
Flexible relational databases
Data import through File Upload, API, and FTP
Recurring import/export jobs through automated data workflows



#### Single Customer/Prospect View

Unify customer user data from all systems and digital touchpoints to get a holistic view of all your known and anonymous customers.
Get all the contact information as well as behavioral insights, permission changes, events and a history of all the campaign sends and engagement for all marketing and transactional communications.




#### Advanced Segmentation


Create marketing segments based on transactional, behavioral, demographic, campaign response history and unique customer attributes. Our intuitive, drop-down segment builder allows marketers to easily build and customize precise segments to specific business needs.
Use rich data from the single customer view and define segments based on any combination of user attributes, site activity, affinities, transaction history, campaign response history and other custom data attributes.
Build thousands of segments without worrying about storage or speed concerns. All segments stay up-to-date in real-time and continuously adapt to customer data.
Remotely segment and filter data from other sources with on-the-fly data connections with your Data Warehouse, CRM System or any other data source.
- Synch your customer segments with social ad platforms directly from the Dengage UI. All customer changes in a segment will automatically be added and/or removed after the initial synch and be kept up-to-date across solutions.














### AI and Customer Driven Journeys


Make the shift from channel-driven to customer-driven marketing by coordinating multi-channel, multi-device experiences. Easily design and launch automated customer journeys using our intuitive, drag-and-drop canvas. Trigger timely messages across channels with relevant content that responds to customer behaviors, context and activity.


**Build and optimize your journeys with Predictive Segments, Channel and Send Time Optimizer.**




### Omnichannel Execution


Use our native channels to execute omnichannel campaigns and measure their success. Get real-time insights on how your customers engage with your brand across channels to optimize your marketing strategy and budget. The current channels provided within Dengage include :

#### Email Marketing


- Personalization: Dengage enables highly personalized email marketing campaigns, allowing businesses to tailor content, subject lines, and offers based on individual customer preferences, behaviors, and demographics.

- Segmentation: Users can segment their email lists in Dengage based on various criteria such as location, purchase history, engagement level, and more. This segmentation capability ensures that emails are targeted to specific audience segments for increased relevance and effectiveness.

- Automations: Dengage offers powerful automation capabilities for email marketing, allowing users to set up triggered email campaigns based on user actions or events such as abandoned carts, product purchases, birthdays, or subscription renewals. This helps in delivering timely and relevant messages to customers throughout their journey.

- A/B Testing: Users can conduct A/B tests within Dengage to optimize email marketing campaigns. This feature allows businesses to test different subject lines, content variations, or sending times to determine which combination performs best in terms of open rates, click-through rates, and conversions.

- Dynamic Content: Dengage supports dynamic content in emails, enabling users to create personalized email experiences by dynamically inserting content blocks or product recommendations based on user data and preferences.

- Responsive Design: Emails created using Dengage are designed to be responsive, ensuring they display correctly and are optimized for various devices and screen sizes, including desktops, tablets, and smartphones. This helps in maximizing engagement and conversion rates across different platforms.


- Performance Analytics: Dengage provides detailed analytics and reporting tools to track the performance of email marketing campaigns in real-time. Users can monitor metrics such as open rates, click-through rates, conversions, revenue generated, and more, allowing them to measure the effectiveness of their efforts and make data-driven decisions for optimization.

- Compliance and Deliverability: Dengage helps users maintain compliance with email marketing regulations such as GDPR and CAN-SPAM by providing features like opt-in/opt-out management, unsubscribe links, and compliance checks. Additionally, Dengage employs best practices to ensure high deliverability rates for emails, minimizing the risk of them being marked as spam.

- Advanced Features: Dengage offers advanced features for email marketing such as predictive analytics, AI-driven recommendations, and multi-channel orchestration, allowing businesses to create sophisticated and personalized customer experiences across email and other communication channels.




#### SMS Marketing


- Subscriber management: Dengage allows businesses to manage their subscriber lists easily. This includes adding new subscribers, organizing them into different segments based on demographics or behavior, and managing opt-ins and opt-outs.

- Personalization: Personalization is key in SMS marketing, and Dengage provides features to personalize messages based on subscriber data. This could include using the subscriber's name, past purchase history, or location to tailor messages for maximum impact.

- Automated campaigns: Dengage offers automation features to help businesses save time and effort in their SMS marketing campaigns. This could include setting up automated welcome messages for new subscribers, sending out birthday messages, or triggering messages based on specific actions or events.

- Scheduling: Dengage allows businesses to schedule their SMS messages in advance. This could be useful for planning out campaigns ahead of time or ensuring that messages are sent at the optimal time for maximum engagement.

- Analytics and reporting: Dengage provides analytics and reporting features to help businesses track the performance of their SMS campaigns. This could include open rates, click-through rates, conversion rates, and more, allowing businesses to optimize their campaigns for better results.

- Compliance and regulations: SMS marketing is subject to various regulations, such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) in the United States. Dengage includes features to help businesses stay compliant with these regulations, such as opt-in and opt-out management and consent tracking.

#### Web Push & On-site Notifications

Cross-platform support: Dengage supports web push notifications across various platforms, including desktop and mobile devices, ensuring broad reach for your notifications.
Personalization: You can personalize notifications based on user attributes such as location, language, browsing behavior, and more. This allows you to deliver targeted and relevant messages to your audience.
Automated messaging: You can set up automated campaigns to send notifications based on user actions or predefined triggers. This includes welcome messages, abandoned cart reminders, re-engagement campaigns, and more, helping you automate your marketing efforts and increase user engagement.
Rich media support: You can include images, emojis, and other rich media elements in your notifications to make them more visually appealing and engaging for users.
Advanced analytics: Dengage provides detailed analytics and reporting tools to track the performance of your notifications. You can monitor metrics such as delivery rate, open rate, click-through rate, conversion rate, and more, to evaluate the effectiveness of your campaigns and make data-driven decisions.
On-site Notifications: Dengage provides out of the box templates to design your Lead Generation forms, NPS forms, Sticky Bars, Native Surveys (can also use our integration with Survey Monkey and Typeforms) and many more along with the flexibility to design your own layout using the HTML editor to engage with users in real-time on your website




#### Mobile Push, In-App and Inbox Notifications


- Instant Delivery: Push notifications are delivered instantly to users' mobile devices, ensuring timely communication.

- Personalization: They can be personalized based on user preferences, behavior, location, or other relevant data, increasing their relevance and effectiveness.

- Rich Media Support: Push notifications can include various types of rich media content such as images, videos, GIFs, or interactive elements, enhancing user engagement.

- Customization: Developers can customize the appearance and behavior of push notifications to match the branding and style of the mobile app, maintaining consistency in user experience.

- Actionable: Push notifications can include actionable buttons or deep links, allowing users to directly interact with the app or perform specific actions without opening it.

- In-App Notifications: In-app notifications are messages that appear while a user is actively using the mobile app. They can be used to provide contextual information, updates, or alerts without interrupting the user experience.

- Mobile Inbox Notifications: Mobile inbox notifications are messages that are stored within the app and can be accessed by users at their convenience. They provide a centralized location for important messages, offers, or updates, allowing users to refer back to them later.

- Geolocation Targeting: Push notifications can be targeted based on the user's geographic location, allowing developers to send location-specific messages or promotions relevant to the user's current whereabouts.

#### Location Based Notifications â€“ Geofencing


Create Geofence Clusters to analyze the number of people who enter and exits pre-defined geographies. Use Geofences to also trigger notifications when your target audience enters, exits or dwells in a given location.




#### WhatsApp Notifications


- WhatsApp Messaging: Dengage allows businesses to send personalized messages to customers via WhatsApp. This could include transactional messages, promotional offers, order updates, etc.
- Campaign Management: Businesses can create and manage WhatsApp marketing campaigns directly within the Dengage platform. This includes scheduling messages, segmenting audiences, and tracking campaign performance.
- Template Messaging: WhatsApp Business API requires the use of approved message templates for certain types of communications, such as order confirmations or shipping updates. Dengage provides tools for managing and using these templates effectively.
- Automation and Workflows: Dengage offers automation capabilities for WhatsApp messaging, allowing businesses to set up workflows based on customer behavior or triggers. For example, sending a welcome message to new subscribers or following up with abandoned cart reminders.

- Analytics and Reporting: Dengage provides insights into the performance of WhatsApp campaigns, including metrics like open rates, click-through rates, conversions, etc. This data can help businesses optimize their messaging strategies.
- Multimedia Messaging: Businesses can send not only text but also multimedia content like images, videos, or documents through WhatsApp using Dengage. This can enhance the engagement and effectiveness of marketing campaigns.
- Personalization: Dengage enables businesses to personalize WhatsApp messages based on customer data and preferences. This can help improve relevance and engagement with customers.



#### Social Re-targeting and 3rd party DMP Sync


- Facebook Custom Audience: provides out-of-the-box integration between Dengage and Facebook to allow marketers to retarget users on Facebook based on behavior captured on Dengage. Using this, marketers can sync users from Dengage to Facebook's custom audience and create ads for this custom audience on Facebook.
- Google Customer Match: Google Ads Audience Sync aims to help marketers improve ROI on their Google Ads spending by allowing them to send Ads to the right audiences. Marketers can retarget the users who have evinced interest in their brand or product but haven't acted (such as subscribing or making a purchase) on their interest.
- TikTok Audience Export: This functionality allows you to integrate your audience segments into TikTok's advertising ecosystem.
- Criteo Audience Sync: Dynamically sync your first-party user data from Dengage directly into Criteo for ad retargeting, lookalike modeling, and suppression. With the Audience Sync to Criteo, you can keep your customer experiences personalized across channels while optimizing ad spend.
- Bluekai: Highly targeted segments/audiences can be created directly in the Dengage UI, and then passed to BlueKai. Data is passed as custom â€œsegment labels.â€ Any data returned from these can be tagged back onto the customer profile, including interaction data (such as clicks, views, etc.), and used to influence further triggered communications.


### Error Logging and Altering


Dengageâ€™s Platform logs and triggers an email to platforms users (based on their role) for any errors that have occurred during campaigns, segmentations, automations, data import/export, etc.

### User Access Roles and Permissions


Dengage provides an easy to use interface to control and define the various roles and permissions that can be granted to the various users accessing the platform.


### Analytics and Reporting


Dengage Analytics provides a holistic view of your omnichannel marketing performance, giving you the ability to see what works and what doesnâ€™t faster than ever. Weâ€™ve gathered all the analytics you need in one place so you can compare channels, measure impact of your efforts and optimize your marketing strategy in real-time.

## PROJECT IMPLEMENTATION METHODOLOGY



### Onboarding and Implementation Strategy


The Dengage Project Management team follows a thorough onboarding and implementation methodology to ensure its clientâ€™s onboarding success. This team is comprised of technical, strategy, and product experts who have years of experience in implementing Dengage Products. Dengageâ€™s onboarding approach is comprised of the following phases (some of which are performed in parallel):









**Phase 1 â€“ Initiation and Planning**

The Dengage Project Management team will partner with you to ensure that the implementation of your Customer Driven Marketing Platform is perfectly configured for your specific marketing and business goals. The initial phase of the onboarding project is to spend time working with the client to properly analyze all the necessary technical and business requirements for the specific implementation.

**Initial Kickoff Meeting and Requirements Gathering**

The Project Management will start with a kickoff meeting with the client to evaluate the needs and requirements for the business.
Typical aspects that are discussed in this phase include:
Current data model
Current programs and campaigns
Segment discovery
Problem areas
Long-term programs and campaigns
Overall strategy and Key Performance Indicators
Unsubscribe management
Integrations with existing platforms/systems and 3rd party solution

**Wizz Air deliverables in the Initiation and Planning phase include:**


Key technical and business contacts
Operational procedures and requirements
Wizz Air change management process
Back-up procedures and requirements
Security policies and procedures
Dengage realizes that all processes and procedures are not always documented. Where necessary, Dengage will work with the appropriate Wizz Air staff to capture the required information. Dengage will use the captured information to produce the following list of deliverables, which will then be integrated into a cohesive set of policies, procedures, and operational documentation.


**Main tasks and deliverables in the Initiation and Planning phase include:**


Detailed implementation plan listing all tasks, milestones and accountable parties
Business Requirements Document
Scope of Work document
Review and clarify design documents
Test plans and procedures for verifying all configurations
Success criteria for determining a successful implementation
Process to hand-off from Implementation team to Operations team










**Phase 2 â€“ Setup and Installation**


The setup and installation phase consist of setting up your accounts, access configuration with MFA and IP restrictions, data flow setup, SDK setup and testing.


**The main tasks in this stage include:**


Account Setup
Review and migrate existing campaigns
Plan and create new campaigns and personalized experiences
Data flow configuration and implementation
Web and Mobile SDK setup and testing

**Creating new Campaigns and Building a Personalization Roadmap:**

- Define overall contact strategy and build a personalization roadmap
- Define data/targeting strategy
- Define Sales Pipeline Management steps and KPIs
- Prioritize quick win campaigns and automated personalized engagement opportunities
- Design, build, and test batch and triggered (Business as Usual) campaigns
- Personalized experience and AI-based segmentation workshops

**Phase 3 â€“ Training and Testing**


Upon completion of the implementation phase, it is expected that your account to be fully configured. This will be verified by executing the previously developed test plans from the above phases to validate application functionality.

The Dengage Project Management team will provide development and end-user manuals to Wizz Air.Â Technical and end-user training will also be organized at this stage. Additionally, a Pilot will be deployed at the end of this stage before deployment. The Project results and executive summary will be prepared and delivered to the Wizz Air team.



**Key deliverables in this phase include:**



**Testing**

System testing
User acceptance testing


**Running the Pilot**

Run Pilot campaigns across channels
Deliver and present project results & executive summary

**Documentation and Training**

System development documents
Development & end-user manuals
Technical and end-user training






**Phase 4 â€“ Deployment and Go Live**


Once all testing has been completed, the steady-state migration of the live application data will begin. The exact methodology used to carry out this task will be determined during the initial planning phase and will include appropriate rollback procedures should an unexpected event occur.
After Go-Live, the following work will be required on an ongoing basis. Again, Dengage can help with these activities through a collaborative model or Wizz Air can drive these activities directly through the interface.
- Build and setup of automated campaigns as defined in the contact strategy and personalization roadmap
- Monitoring, testing, and optimization of campaigns and personalized experiences
- Adding/optimization of data synchronization
- Check all reports for revenue generation, deliverability, etc.

Access to Dengageâ€™s online customer service portal to create support tickets.
Email assistance for product-related technical issues during Dengageâ€™s working hours on a seven-day-a-week basis.
Out-of-hours support
Account management

**Response and Resolution Service Levels**

Response means positive acknowledgment from Dengage that Dengage has received information from the end user regarding a question with the Software and that Dengage is investigating the details. Resolution means the closure of a customer issue. Detailed SLA are covered in Annex 1

### Dengageâ€™s Risk Management Approach




Each step is detailed below;
Risk Management Planningâ€”The initial work performed to identify the risk management approach to be used on the project.
Risk Identificationâ€” The potential sources of risks both initially and on an ongoing basis identified.
Qualitative Risk Assessmentâ€”The process of actually assessing the risks against the project assessment criteria.
Quantitative Risk Assessmentâ€”The process of budgeting the risks against the project assessment criteria.
Risk Response Planningâ€”The creative process of identifying the risk response strategies that will be used and the detailed risk response plan(s) (what will be done or not done) for each risk identified. This planning includes identifying the trigger event that will cause the risk response plan to be executed.
Risk Monitoring and Controlâ€”The process of monitoring for a risk event occurrence, reassessing the risk (likelihood and consequence) and monitoring the performance of the risk response plan and reporting the results.

## Company Profile



### About Dengage


Dengage is the brainchild of Aman Dotani and Osman TufanoÄŸullarÄ±, both of whom are pioneers and shapers of the MarTech industry since the early 2000â€™s. Between them they have organically grown, transformed, co-founded and headed independent leaders of our industry: Emailvision (SmartFocus); Emarsys (SAP); euromessage/Related Digital (Dogus Group); transforming them from small parochial providers into global multi-million-dollar overachievers subsequently bought by much larger corporations. Spotting a gap in the MarTech environment and wishing to maintain the flexibility and agility of being an independent provider, the Dengage company was established in early 2018.

The team devoted close to 15 months in pure development, entirely self-funded, before seriously launching the platform in 2019. Thanks to the pedigree of the technology, our first clients were heavy hitters such as Akbank, N11.com, Sportsmaster.ru whose success with us allowed further expansion into the markets of Turkey, Russia, LATAM, APAC, Middle East and Africa. 2020 and subsequently 2021 has seen the business ramp up significantly in turnover, team size and client base. 

We currently have almost 100 team members worldwide lead by hugely experienced industry experts allowing us to further expand the business into Europe, and North America. Serving more than 130 clients from our registered offices in London, Moscow, Istanbul, Dubai, and Islamabad we continue to grow exponentially and completely organically without the need for additional investment. We are looking towards late 2022 for our first series A, at which point we expect the business to be valued at ten times its current valuation.

### Annex-2 (Standard GDPR Compliance)


- Introduction to the GDPR
Consumer data protection and privacy have always been important concerns for organizations that process personal customer data. Responding to the increased incidence of data breaches worldwide, governments are also focusing on consumer data security in the form of new legislation. Organizations are required to comply with these continuously evolving regulations, while staying innovative and developing their products and services.
The General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) is European Union legislation that represents the most significant change to data protection laws in the EU since 1995. The GDPR replaces that yearâ€™s Data Protection Directive (Directive 95/46/EC) and unites data protection regulations across the entire EU.
The GDPR presents significant challenges for any organization doing business in Europe or with EU citizens, regardless of where the organization is located. The GDPR specifies non-compliance fines at 4% of a companyâ€™s annual global revenue or â‚¬20 million, whichever is greater. As no organization wants to face huge fines or negative impact on their reputation among customers, investors and employees, compliance with the GDPR has received much attention by organizations of all sizes.
Your customers need to know that they can trust you with their personal data, and trust that you are taking the necessary measures to ensure the security of this information. If an organization fails to comply with the GDPR, it may indicate to the market that the organization is not doing everything it must to keep its customersâ€™ data secure. When this level of trust is missing, it can lead to the loss of customers and employees, drops in share price and other hazards of a tarnished reputation.

- How the GDPR Applies to DÂ·engage
The GDPR defines two main entities: â€œdata processorâ€ and â€œdata controllerâ€. The definitions of, relationships between and responsibilities of data controllers and data processors are specified with the goal of protecting the rights of EU citizens whose personal data is stored or processed. The GDPR describes the particular types of security, IT and administrative activities that might be considered necessary for handling personal data, including encryption, documentation and taking measures to ensure the integrity, confidentiality, availability, resilience, assessment and post-incident-recovery of processing systems and services.

**In our business relationship with you, DÂ·engage acts as a data processor, while your company is a data controller.**

Data controller (You) â€“ Controllers bear the primary responsibility for GDPR compliance. As a data controller, you manage customer data, access controls and authentication.
Data processor (DÂ·engage) â€“ Any entity that processes personal data under the controllerâ€™s instructions. As a data processor, we provide cloud-based software, services, infrastructure and support. As a data processor, we are obliged to make our best efforts to protect your customer data and enable you to fully meet GDPR requirements.
- Prepared for the GDPR
At DÂ·engage, we are committed to full compliance with everything required by the GDPR, as well as taking additional precautions to ensure the complete security and privacy of your customersâ€™ data. DÂ·engage has always focused on data protection and regulatory compliance, and we are constantly looking for better ways to secure data in transit, at rest and in use. We strive to meet the demands of our client base of 100+ companies from more than 25 countries to comply with all relevant regional laws and regulations.
We have prepared for GDPR by actively enhancing our products to ensure that your organization meets the data privacy, protection, confidentiality, integrity and availability challenges that the GDPR places on the data that we handle for your organization.
These preparations have included low-level mapping of PII, data privacy vision and strategy, data protection impact assessments, employee awareness and training, data breach management planning and others.
DÂ·engage operates across 4 data centers â€“ Frankfurt, Germany, Moscow, Russia, Istanbul, Turkey and Ohio, US â€“ giving you the choice of where your data is stored and processed.


- How DÂ·engage Complies with the GDPR
The following table presents the specific ways in which DÂ·engage complies with the requirements of the GDPR.


- Frequently Asked Questions about DÂ·engage and the GDPR

**Is DÂ·engage GDPR compliant?**

Yes, as explained above in this document.

**Is DÂ·engage a Data Processor or a Data Controller?**

As regards your customer data, DÂ·engage is a data processor, while you are a data controller. As your data processor, we are obliged to make our best efforts to protect your customer data and enable you to fully meet the relevant GDPR requirements.

**Does DÂ·engage have a data center in Europe?**

Yes, DÂ·engage operates a data center in Frankfurt, Germany.
DÂ·engage will not transfer the storage or processing of your customersâ€™ personal data outside the country where it is hosted, in conformance with your instructions as the data controller.

**What data am I allowed to collect from EU citizens and what data am I not allowed to collect?**

The GDPR does not limit the types of personal data that can be collected. Instead, the GDPR requires organizations that collect such data to demonstrate â€œData Security by Designâ€ â€“ a dedicated policy that evidences sound principles of security designed to protect personal data from unauthorized access, use or other types of processing.


### Annex-3 (Standard Security Policy)


- GENERAL SECURITY
Dengage and its management is committed to establishing and maintaining secure environments in which it conducts business. The following security policies define Dengage's approach to managing security.

**1.1	Scope of Policies**

These policies apply to all operations, employees, information handled and computer and data communication systems owned by or administered by the Company Examples of what these policies cover would include:
â—	All business systems involved in collecting, processing, maintaining, transmitting or reporting of both Company and customer
â—	Network infrastructure components, which support and provide access to these business or product

**1.2	Risks to be Protected Against**

Information is an asset, which, like other important business assets, has significant value to Dengage and consequently needs to be suitably protected. Dengage, as part of its on-going program to maintain adequate and effective internal controls, seeks to ensure that itâ€™s Information Systems (e.g. devices, operating systems, applications, and, most importantly, its proprietary company and customer information), are adequately protected to reduce the risk of loss and security breaches due to:
â—	Intentional acts by individuals inside or outside the Company;
â—	Inappropriate access by individuals or groups untrained in correct Company policies or procedures;
â—	Accidental loss of a portable device (such as a laptop) containing confidential or proprietary Company information; and
â—	Accidents, natural disasters or another force majeure
This document represents the Companyâ€™s Security Policies. It may be supplemented by human resources but should be considered as minimal security requirements to be met by all employees. Additionally, some customer specific situations may require more detailed or more stringent policies in which case these policies will be documented and attached as an addendum to this document covering those operations.
If there is any conflict between any Security Policies or laws and regulations applicable to a particular business unit, employees should comply with the most restrictive requirement.


**1.3	What is Being Protected**

These Security Policies seek to protect systems as defined in the Policy Scope:
â—	All desktop computers, servers, data storage devices, communication systems, routers, switches, hubs, and other information system devices owned or leased by the Company (collectively, â€œComputing Platformsâ€);
â—	Any Computing Platforms, operating system software, middleware, or application software under the control of third parties that connect in any way to our computer networks;
â—	All operating systems which provide the foundation for our Information Systems;
â—	All proprietary software developed by the Company for internal and external use or any software developed by a third party specifically for the Companyâ€™s internal use;
â—	All third party â€œshrink-wrappedâ€ application software licensed by the Company and running on any of its Computing Platforms;
â—	All open-source software legally licensed by the Company and running on any of its Computing Platforms; and
â—	Any and all data, information, knowledge, documents, presentations, databases, graphics, or other intellectual property stored on the Companyâ€™s Computing Platforms; this includes, but is not limited to, electronically stored, printed, or faxed Information and information systems must be protected from unauthorized access, disclosure, use, modification or destruction in a manner consistent with their value, sensitivity and criticality. It is intended to cover all corporate information and systems regardless of their physical location, the media on which it is stored, the systems that process it or the methods by which it is moved or transmitted.

**1.4	Who These Policies Apply To**

These Policies apply worldwide to:
â—	All full and part-time employees of the Company;
â—	All exempt and nonexempt employees of the Company;
â—	All affiliated third parties and contractors who work on the Companyâ€™s premises or who remotely connect their computing platforms to the Companyâ€™s computing


**1.5	Major Goals of These Security Policies**

The major goals of the Dengage Security Policies are:
â—	To provide an overall framework, to guide Company personnel when implementing network security policies and procedures for the Companyâ€™s computing platforms and other information assets;
â—	To provide guidance to all employees on the proper handling of Company Confidential Information;
â—	To provide a basis for security and confidentiality training and educational awareness programs developed by the Company;
â—	To provide the Companyâ€™s IT staff with the framework necessary to convert the Companyâ€™s Info Security Policy to implementation standards for each platform, operating system, application, and security device that can then be monitored and enforced against the policies.

**1.6	Data Classification**

Data is defined as the collection of information assets compiled, generated or maintained to support the business.
This data will be classified and handled according to the Dengage Data Classification Policy and Dengage Data Handling Standard.

- SECURITY POLICY STANDARDS AND PROCEDURES

**3.1	Information Security Policy Document(s)**

Dengage shall at all times maintain Security Policies, which define the Information Security Policies for the Company.
Management is responsible for approval of Dengageâ€™s Security Policies. These Policies shall be published and communicated to all employees of the Company and to others whom these Policies apply. These Policies demonstrate managementâ€™s commitment and set out the approach to managing information security.
These policies should be communicated throughout the Company to users in a form that is relevant, accessible and understandable to the intended reader.


**3.2	Maintenance, Review and Evaluation of Policies**

Development, maintenance and review of these Security Policies are the responsibility  of  management. These Security Policies may be revised from time to
time in response to changes such as significant security incidents, new vulnerabilities or changes to the Company or its technical infrastructure.
To ensure the ongoing relevance of the Policy, management will periodically reassess:
â—	The Security policiesâ€™ effectiveness, as demonstrated by the nature, number, and impact of recorded security incidents;
â—	The cost and impact of controls on business efficiency; and
â—	Effects of changes to relevant security standards and compliance and regulatory compliance.

**3.3	Evidence of Compliance with Policies**

Business Units will be required to provide evidence to senior management that the controls described in this document have been implemented and executed. The evidence must be retained for a minimum of two (2) years. Management may provide further guidance about retention of evidence. Evidence may take the form of hand written, printed, and/or electronic documents.
Controls may be evidenced in many ways depending on the specific control, consult with Security and Compliance for more information.

**3.4	Exceptions to Policies**

Where it is considered not feasible to comply with Security Policies, exceptions should be documented and initially approved within the business unit. Exceptions should then be forwarded by the senior manager for the business unit to Security and Compliance or final approval.


- ORGANIZATIONAL SECURITY PROCESSES
Dengage Leadership believes that information security is a business responsibility shared by all of its employees. It is the Companyâ€™s. intention, through the creation, maintenance and implementation of these Security Policies, that employees shall be trained in, updated on and empowered to implement those responsibilities within their department/business unit.

**4.1	Corporate and Business Unit Security Responsibilities**

The following roles/titles and associate responsibilities/user privileges are defined as part of this Security Policy document:
Security Officer Responsibilities include:

â—	Ensuring that all the Companyâ€™s Information Processing Systems have appropriate policies, procedures, personnel and equipment in place to ensure the integrity and confidentiality of all information residing on those systems;
â—	Setting security standards for the Company;
â—	Managing the security of shared network components not owned by any division business unit;
â—	Managing the Companyâ€™s IT/Security personnel;
â—	Instituting best security practices, the Company or its external auditors for Information Technology;
â—	Ensuring that business unit security programs appropriately address security across different functional departments.
IT/Technology Security Management/Personnel Responsibilities include:
