## EXECUTIVE SUMMARY


Dengage (Dengage Limited) is a Private Limited Company that started its operations in January 2018. It is owned by Aman Dotani (50%) and Itri Osman (50%) and provides a Customer Data and Engagement Platform.
The Dengage platform provides an enterprise-ready cross-channel personalized engagement platform that marketers love and the whole organization trusts. MCB Bankâ€™s marketing can target the right audience across channels, grow revenue, and build customer relationships that â€˜make truly great things happen.â€™
With the Dengage Customer Driven Marketing Platform (CDMP) MCB Bank will be able to empower its sales and marketing teams to lead the customer experience by centralizing all email marketing efforts under one platform. The Dengage Customer Driven Marketing Platform will empower MCB Bank to deliver a world-class customer experience by unifying your data, engaging the right audience, and analyzing performance across all email campaigns.
In addition to providing MCB Bank with a rich set of out-of-the-box Email Platform, Dengage Customer Driven Marketing Driven Platformâ€™s data-agnostic approach, integration capabilities, and AI/Machine Learning capabilities suppresses the complexity of the marketing ecosystem. This will allow MCB Bank to easily use all available data in the organization to improve the sales pipeline, boost productivity, and improve customer satisfaction by delivering personalized and seamless emails.





## INTRODUCTION



### Dengageâ€™s Customer Engagement Platform â€“ where Big Data meets Marketing Automation

Dengage's open data architecture in its Customer Data Platform (CDP) is a crucial aspect for MCB Bank, especially considering the ever-evolving landscape of the banking industry and the growing technological advancements. The extensibility of Dengage's open data architecture in its Customer Data Platform presents a future-proof solution for MCB Bank. It not only accommodates the current operational and email marketing needs but also provides a robust foundation for growth, innovation, and adaptation to future trends and technologies. This adaptability is key in the dynamic banking sector, positioning MCB Bank to leverage data-driven insights and strategies effectively, both now and in the future. Our Unique Value Propositions for MCB Bank :
Data Connection and IT Integration - Our integration process is streamlined, and capable of assimilating all your data within standard databases through a singular connection. This seamless integration negates the need for additional IT department efforts. We can utilize customer data residing in data warehouses and databases such as Oracle, PostgreSQL, MS SQL, MySQL, SAP HANA, IBM DB2, Google Big Cloud, and Amazon Redshift through configurable procedures, like setting access to read-only aggregated data sets, without necessitating replication to our local Customer Data Platform (CDP).
Scalable Customer Data Platform and Marketing Automation - Our services are trusted by a multitude of clients across various sectors, notably for our scalable Customer Data Platform (CDP) and marketing automation that can be deployed on-premises (at an additional cost) or cloud (like AWS) or Local Cloud in Pakistan (at an additional cost).
Segmentation Capabilities - Unlike any other competitors who cannot segment based on unprocessed data, our platform excels. Conventionally, a segment must be predefined, thereafter data begins to be associated with itâ€”this presents a substantial drawback for dynamic, data-driven marketing strategies. Our service is unparalleled, allowing for the creation of segments within vast data sets comprising millions of customers, with immediate and retrospective data accumulation.
Multi-Channel Messaging Services â€“ Apart from the present requirement for an email platform, Dengage will be also able to gradually help MCB Bank to scale to other channels like Web Push Notifications, Mobile Push Notifications, In-App Notifications, WhatsApp, Web Onsite Notifications, etc. along with expanding customer reach outs to Facebook Customer Audience, Google Customer Match, TikTok Audience Match â€“ all in 1 single out-of-the-box platform.
Local Presence and Support â€“ Headquartered in the United Kingdom with a global team of 140+ employees, Dengage has 20+ team member team based in Pakistan across Islamabad, Lahore, and Karachi that helps us in supporting our regional operations in Pakistan with dedicated technical support and account management. Along with this, Dengage is a trusted partner for enterprise customers like Allied Bank Limited, MCB Funds, UBL Funds, JS Bank, Nishat Linen, Sapphire Retail, and many more.

## DENGAGE RESPONSE TO SCOPE OF WORK

In this section, we have briefly addressed each one of the objectives listed by the MCB Bank team in the RFP. The following sections contain more detailed information about Dengageâ€™s Email Marketing Platform.

### Goal #1 - User-Friendly Email Editor


Dengage Capabilities: Dengage's email marketing platform offers a user-friendly email editor that allows users to easily create visually appealing emails without any coding knowledge. The editor provides a drag-and-drop interface, making it simple to add images, text, buttons, and other elements to emails. Users can choose from a variety of pre-designed templates or customize their own to suit their brand's unique style. Additionally, the platform includes features such as responsive design, real-time preview, and an intuitive interface to streamline the email creation process. With Dengage's email editor, users can quickly create professional-looking emails that engage their audience effectively. Along with the drag-and-drop editor, Dengage also provides an HTML email editor too.




### Goal #2 - Personalization and Dynamic Content

Dengage Capabilities: Dengage's email marketing platform excels in personalization and dynamic content. It leverages user data to tailor email content to each recipient's preferences, behavior, and demographics. Through advanced segmentation and targeting capabilities, marketers can create highly relevant and personalized email campaigns. The platform also supports dynamic content insertion, allowing marketers to dynamically change email elements based on factors like user interactions or real-time data. This results in more engaging and effective email communications that drive better results and higher conversions.

Dengageâ€™s platform is easily able to create engaging and personalized emails across various use cases shared by MCB Bank incl Promotional Emails, Product Announcements, Newsletter Campaigns,  Event Promotions, Customer Surveys and Feedback, Welcome Emails, Birthday and Anniversary Emails, Re-engagement Campaigns, Loyalty Program Updates, Company Newsletters, Educational Content, Emergency Notifications, Policy Updates, and many more.


### Goal #3 - Segmentation and Targeting


Dengage Capabilities: Create marketing segments based on transactional, behavioral, demographic, campaign response history, and unique customer attributes. Our intuitive, drop-down segment builder allows marketers to easily build and customize precise segments to specific business needs.
Use rich data from the single customer view and define segments based on any combination of user attributes, site activity, affinities, transaction history, campaign response history, and other custom data attributes.
Build thousands of segments without worrying about storage or speed concerns. All segments stay up-to-date in real time and continuously adapt to customer data.
Remotely segment and filter data from other sources with on-the-fly data connections with your Data Warehouse, CRM System, or any other data source.
- Synchronize your customer segments with social ad platforms directly from the Dengage UI. All customer changes in a segment will automatically be added and/or removed after the initial synch and be kept up-to-date across solutions.



### Goal #4 - Marketing Automation


Dengage Capabilities: Dengage's email marketing platform offers powerful automation capabilities to streamline your marketing efforts. With Dengage, you can create automated email campaigns triggered by specific actions or events, such as user sign-ups or dormancy. This allows you to send targeted and timely messages to your audience, increasing engagement and conversions. Additionally, Dengage provides advanced segmentation tools to personalize your emails based on subscriber data, ensuring that each recipient receives relevant content. Overall, Dengage's automation features help you save time, improve efficiency, and drive better results for your email marketing campaigns.


### Goal #5 - A/B Testing and Optimization

Dengage Capabilities: Dengage's A/B testing feature allows users to compare different versions of their email campaigns to determine which one performs better. Users can test different subject lines, content, images, or send times to optimize email marketing efforts. Dengage provides detailed reports and analytics to help users understand which variations are most effective in engaging their audience. This helps businesses make data-driven decisions to improve their email marketing strategy and increase conversion rates. Overall, Dengage's A/B testing capability empowers users to refine their campaigns and achieve better results.



### Goal #6 - Advanced Analytics and Reporting

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage will allow MCB Bank to create, send, and track their email marketing campaigns. It provides advanced analytics and reporting capabilities that can track key metrics such as open rates, click-through rates, and conversion rates. With these insights, the marketing teams can measure the effectiveness of their email campaigns and make data-driven decisions to optimize their marketing efforts. Dengage also offers features such as A/B testing, segmentation, and automation, allowing marketing teams to target specific customer segments with personalized content and automate their email workflows for improved efficiency. Additionally, Dengage integrates with other banking systems and tools, enabling seamless data exchange and a more comprehensive view of customer interactions across different channels.
Dengage Provides various reports out of the box like :
- Email Campaign Summarized Report

- Email Domain Distribution Report

- Email Click Report
- Email Heatmap Analysis
- Overall Campaigns Report

### Goal #7 - Email Deliverability Tools

Dengage Solution Capabilities: One of our key capabilities is our Email Deliverability, which helps ensure that emails reach recipients' inboxes rather than being marked as spam. These tools include features such as authentication protocols (like SPF, DKIM, and DMARC), inbox placement testing, and monitoring of sender reputation to improve the chances of emails being successfully delivered and opened by recipients. Additionally, Dengage offers dedicated IP addresses, which can further improve deliverability by separating your email reputation from other senders, and list cleaning services to remove invalid or inactive email addresses, reducing bounce rates and improving sender reputation overall.



### Goal #8 - Responsive Templates and Mobile Optimization

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage's email marketing platform offers Responsive Templates and Mobile Optimization features to ensure that your emails look great on any device. These templates are designed to automatically adjust their layout and formatting to fit the screen size of the device being used, whether it's a desktop, tablet, or smartphone.
Mobile Optimization ensures that your emails are optimized specifically for mobile devices, taking into account factors such as smaller screen sizes and touch-based navigation. This ensures that your emails load quickly and are easy to interact with on mobile devices, increasing the likelihood that recipients will engage with your content.
Also, with our multi-screen preview functionality, marketing teams will be able to preview the email on 15+ different devices before launching the campaign.



### Goal #9 - Integration Capabilities


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage offers comprehensive integration possibilities like APIs, SFTP, Manual Import, and remote data connectivity solutions that enable seamless integration with various database systems, including Microsoft SQL Server, Oracle DB, PostgreSQL, SAP HANA, AWS RedShift, Google Big Query, and other popular database platforms. Our platform is designed to facilitate easy access to your data regardless of its location, ensuring real-time connectivity and efficient data management.

Key features of Dengage's remote data connectivity solution include:

- Universal Compatibility: Dengage is not limited to Microsoft SQL databases; we support a wide range of database systems, including MySQL, PostgreSQL, Oracle, and more. This universal compatibility ensures that you can easily connect to and access data from any database environment within your organization without the need to duplicate data and remotely query the various databases on the fly.

- Secure Data Transmission: Security is paramount when it comes to remote data connectivity. Dengage employs robust encryption protocols and secure transmission channels to safeguard your data during transit, mitigating the risk of unauthorized access or data breaches.

- Flexible Integration Options: Whether you prefer direct database connections, API integrations, or middleware solutions, Dengage offers flexible integration options to suit your specific requirements. Our team of experts will work closely with you to determine the most suitable integration approach based on your infrastructure and business needs.

- Real-time Data Synchronization: Dengage enables real-time data synchronization between disparate database systems, ensuring that your applications and analytics platforms always have access to the most up-to-date information. This real-time synchronization capability eliminates data silos and enhances overall operational efficiency.

- Scalability and Performance: Whether you're dealing with small-scale deployments or large enterprise environments, Dengage's remote data connectivity solution is highly scalable and optimized for performance. Our platform can handle high volumes of data transactions with minimal latency, ensuring smooth and uninterrupted access to your critical business data.

In conclusion, Dengage offers a comprehensive remote data connectivity solution providing universal compatibility, robust security, flexible integration options, real-time data synchronization, and scalability. We are confident that our solution can effectively address your organization's data connectivity needs and help you achieve your business objectives.

### Goal #10 - Advanced Personalization Tags


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Advanced Personalization Tags (known as Customization Selector) enable marketers to dynamically insert personalized content into their email campaigns. These tags can pull in data from various sources, such as a customer's profile, past behavior, or real-time interactions with the brand. This allows marketers to create highly relevant and targeted email messages that resonate with each recipient on a personal level.

Some capabilities of Dengage's Advanced Personalization Tags include:

- Dynamic Content Insertion: Marketers can use personalization tags to insert dynamic content into their emails based on each recipient's unique characteristics or behavior. For example, they can include personalized product recommendations, tailored offers, or relevant content based on past interactions.
- Conditional Logic: Dengage's personalization tags may also support conditional logic, allowing marketers to customize email content based on specific conditions or criteria. This could include showing different content to customers based on their location, purchase history, or engagement level.


### Goal #11 - List Growth Tools


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengageâ€™s platform also provides an out-of-the-box Onsite notifications module that will enable MCB Bank to grow its database and convert anonymous users into registered contacts with the help of :
- Customizable sign-up forms: Dengage allows users to create and customize sign-up forms to capture email addresses and other relevant information from potential subscribers.
- Pop-ups: Dengage offers the ability to create pop-up forms that appear on websites, prompting visitors to subscribe to email lists or promotions.
- Integration capabilities: Dengage seamlessly integrates with various platforms and tools (such as Survey Monkey and Typeform), allowing users to sync subscriber data and track list growth across different channels.
- Analytics and reporting: Dengage provides insights into list growth metrics, such as conversion rates and acquisition sources, enabling users to optimize their strategies for increasing email subscriber lists.

### Goal #12 - Spam and Bounce Handling


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage with its email servers can monitor, capture, and analyze various parameters like open, click, hard bounce, soft bounce, unsubscriptions, spam, etc. (refer to Section 2.6 Image 1) to manage the best possible delivery of email campaigns.

- Advanced filtering: Dengage uses advanced algorithms to filter out spam emails before they are sent to ensure compliance with email regulations.
- Real-time monitoring: The platform continuously monitors email delivery and engagement metrics to identify potential issues such as high bounce rates or spam complaints.
- Bounce handling: Dengage automatically processes bounce notifications from email servers and categorizes them into soft bounces (temporary issues) or hard bounces (permanent issues) to maintain a clean email list.
- Feedback loop integration: Dengage integrates with email service providers' feedback loops to quickly identify and suppress email addresses that generate spam complaints.
- Compliance features: Dengage includes features such as unsubscribe links, GDPR, and CAN-SPAM compliance checks to help prevent emails from being flagged as spam.


### Goal #13 - Scheduled Sending and Time Zone Optimization:


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage provides out-of-the-box send-time optimization and scheduling options for all your email campaigns.

- Scheduled Sending: Dengage's email marketing platform allows users to schedule their email campaigns to be sent at specific dates and times. This feature is helpful for marketers who want to plan their campaigns and ensure that they reach their target audience at the most optimal times for engagement.

- Personalized Timing: Dengage's platform also offers the ability to personalize sending times for individual subscribers based on their past behavior and engagement patterns. This level of customization allows marketers to tailor their email delivery strategy to each subscriber's preferences, maximizing the chances of capturing their attention and driving desired actions.

- A/B Testing: Dengage enables A/B testing of different sending times and frequencies to determine the most effective approach for reaching and engaging with subscribers. By experimenting with various timing options and analyzing the results, marketers can identify the optimal sending schedule that generates the highest response rates and maximizes the ROI of their email marketing efforts.

### Goal #17 - Compliance and Data Protection Tools


Dengage Solution Capabilities: At Dengage, we prioritize data privacy and security to ensure that our platform aligns with regulatory requirements and meets the specific needs of MCB Banks.
Here's how our platform addresses various rights under GDPR / CAN-SPAM and other data protection regulations, as well as Privacy by Design principles:
GDPR Compliance: Dengage is committed to compliance with the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) and other global privacy laws. Our platform is designed to facilitate compliance with various rights granted to individuals under GDPR, including:
Right to Erasure: Dengage enables clients to promptly erase personal data upon request, ensuring compliance with individuals' right to have their data deleted.
Right to Access: Our platform provides mechanisms for individuals to access the personal data stored by our clients, allowing them to review and verify the accuracy of their information.
Right to Rectification: Dengage allows clients to easily update and rectify inaccurate or incomplete personal data, ensuring individuals' right to have their information corrected.
Right to Data Portability: We support data portability by facilitating the transfer of personal data to individuals or third parties in a commonly used and machine-readable format, as required by GDPR.
Right to Restriction of Processing: Our platform includes features to restrict the processing of personal data in certain circumstances, in compliance with individuals' right to restrict processing.
Right to Object: Dengage enables individuals to object to the processing of their data for specific purposes, as provided for by GDPR.
Right to Be Informed: We ensure transparency by providing clear and concise information to individuals about how their data is processed, including purposes, legal basis, and rights available to them.
Opt-Out of Campaign Messages: Our platform offers opt-out mechanisms for individuals to unsubscribe from campaign messages, ensuring compliance with regulations governing direct marketing communications.
Privacy by Design: Dengage incorporates Privacy by Design principles into our platform's architecture and development processes. This includes:
Data Minimization: We encourage clients to collect and process only the minimum amount of personal data necessary for their engagement purposes, reducing the risk of privacy breaches.
Security by Default: Our platform implements robust security measures by default, including encryption, access controls, and authentication mechanisms, to protect personal data against unauthorized access or disclosure.
User Consent Management: Dengage provides tools for managing user consent preferences and obtaining explicit consent where required by law, ensuring lawful and transparent data processing.
Data Lifecycle Management: We assist clients in implementing data retention policies and automated deletion processes to ensure that personal data is retained only for as long as necessary and securely disposed of when no longer needed.
We are fully committed to supporting MCB Bank's requirements for data protection and compliance and would be happy to provide further details or address any specific questions you may have in this regard. Refer to Annex 1 for more details on Dengageâ€™s GDPR Compliance.

## DENGAGE RESPONSE TO TECHNICAL INFRASTRUCTURE


### Goal #1 - Redundant Data Centers

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage utilizes multiple data centers located in different geographic regions to enhance the reliability and availability of its services.
The redundancy provided by these distributed data centers ensures that if one data center encounters problems or goes offline, the system seamlessly transitions to another data center, minimizing downtime for users. This architecture enables Dengage to maintain uninterrupted service delivery even in the event of hardware failures, natural disasters, or other unforeseen circumstances affecting individual data centers.

### Goal #2 - Load Balancing

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage implements load balancing by utilizing a distributed architecture that spreads email-sending tasks across multiple servers. This ensures that no single server becomes overwhelmed with traffic and helps to maintain optimal performance. Through intelligent routing algorithms, Dengage dynamically allocates resources based on server capacity and workload, ensuring that each server receives a fair share of email-sending tasks. This prevents overloads and bottlenecks, allowing for efficient handling of email traffic.
Dengage continuously monitors server performance and adjusts the distribution of email-sending traffic in real-time. By automatically scaling resources up or down as needed, Dengage effectively balances the load across servers, optimizing overall system performance and reliability.

### Goal #3 - Dedicated IP Addresses

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage provides clients with dedicated IP addresses, allowing them to maintain full control over their sender reputation. This means they can manage their email delivery independently and ensure optimal reliability.
By offering dedicated IP addresses, Dengage enables clients to avoid potential issues associated with sharing IPs with other senders. This isolation helps maintain a clean reputation, improving email deliverability and reducing the risk of being flagged as spam. With dedicated IP addresses, clients can implement personalized strategies for email delivery based on their specific needs and preferences. This level of customization empowers them to optimize their email marketing efforts and achieve better results.

### Goal #4 - Server Reliability

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage ensures server reliability by utilizing high-quality enterprise-grade servers. Failover mechanisms are in place to minimize downtime and ensure continuous email-sending capabilities. These failover mechanisms automatically switch to backup servers if the primary servers encounter any issues. This approach enhances the reliability of Dengage's email-sending service, as it reduces the risk of disruptions or outages.
By investing in robust infrastructure and failover mechanisms, Dengage prioritizes maintaining a reliable service for its users.

## DENGAGE RESPONSE TO SCALABILITY



### Goal #1 - Elastic Infrastructure

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage's architecture is built to easily integrate with elastic infrastructure, meaning it can automatically adjust its resource allocation based on fluctuations in email volume and traffic spikes.
The system is designed to be highly scalable, allowing it to efficiently handle increases in workload without manual intervention. This scalability enables Dengage to effectively cater to businesses with varying email marketing needs, ensuring optimal performance during peak times and minimizing downtime. By dynamically allocating resources as needed, Dengage can maintain high availability and responsiveness, even when faced with sudden surges in demand.
Overall, Dengage's approach to elastic infrastructure allows it to deliver a reliable and flexible email marketing solution to its clients, adapting seamlessly to changing requirements and ensuring consistent performance.

### Goal #2 - Auto-scaling

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage is designed to automatically adjust server resources based on fluctuations in email volume. During periods of high demand, such as increased email volumes, Dengage can provide additional servers or resources to ensure smooth handling of the load.
Conversely, when email volumes decrease or during periods of lower demand, Dengage scales down its resources to optimize cost efficiency. This auto-scaling feature allows Dengage to dynamically adapt to changing workload requirements without manual intervention. By efficiently managing resources based on demand, Dengage helps organizations maintain performance while minimizing infrastructure costs.


## DENGAGE RESPONSE TO SECURITY MEASURES


### Goal #1 - Data Encryption

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage ensures data encryption by requiring all data transmission to be encrypted using industry-standard encryption protocols such as TLS/SSL. This encryption helps prevent interception or tampering of email content and user information during transmission.

### Goal #2 - Authentication Protocols


Dengage Solution Capabilities: For Detailed authentication protocols adhered to by Dengage, please Refer to section 3.7


### Goal #3 - Firewalls and Intrusion Detection


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengageâ€™s platform prioritizes security by implementing robust firewall systems and intrusion detection mechanisms. These measures are crucial for protecting against unauthorized access and potential attacks. We have integrated a variety of security devices into our network infrastructure to filter incoming and outgoing customer data effectively. Our approach involves implementing advanced firewall technology that monitors and controls incoming and outgoing network traffic based on predetermined security rules.
In addition to firewall protection, we employ intrusion detection systems (IDS) to further enhance our security posture. These systems continuously monitor network traffic for signs of potential security threats, such as unusual patterns or suspicious behavior. If an intrusion is detected, the IDS will trigger an alert, allowing our security team to investigate and respond promptly.
Furthermore, we regularly update and maintain our security devices to ensure they are equipped to handle emerging threats effectively. This proactive approach helps us stay ahead of potential security risks and safeguard our platform and customers' data against unauthorized access and attacks.

### Goal #4 - Regular Security Audits


Dengage Solution Capabilities: To ensure the safety and integrity of our system, we regularly conduct security audits and penetration testing. These audits are performed by experienced professionals who specialize in identifying and addressing vulnerabilities within our platform. Through these audits, we can proactively identify any potential security risks and take necessary measures to mitigate them.


### Goal #5 - Data Privacy and Compliance


Dengage Solution Capabilities: For Detailed data privacy and compliance protocols adhered to by Dengage, please Refer to section 3.17



### Goal #6 - Anti-Phishing and Anti-Spam Filters

Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengageâ€™s platform utilizes advanced filters and algorithms to effectively combat phishing attempts and spam emails. These filters are designed to identify and block suspicious emails before they even reach the recipients' inboxes. To achieve this, our platform continuously monitors incoming emails for various indicators of phishing and spam, such as suspicious links, attachments, and content patterns commonly associated with fraudulent activity. These indicators are analyzed using sophisticated algorithms that are constantly updated to stay ahead of evolving threats.
Additionally, our platform incorporates machine learning techniques to further enhance its ability to detect and block phishing attempts and spam emails. By analyzing large volumes of data and identifying patterns and trends, our platform can quickly adapt to new and emerging threats, ensuring that our users' inboxes remain protected at all times.


### Goal #7 - Access Control


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengage provides an easy-to-use interface to control and define the various roles and permissions that can be granted to the various users accessing the platform.




### Goal #8 - User Authentication


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Dengageâ€™s platform takes user authentication seriously by providing multi-factor authentication (MFA) as an option. This feature adds an extra layer of security to user accounts, helping to prevent unauthorized access. With MFA enabled users are required to provide two or more forms of verification before gaining access to their accounts, such as a password combined with a one-time code sent to their mobile device or email address. This helps to ensure that even if a password is compromised, attackers would still need access to the additional verification method to gain entry.
Dengage also provides Time-Based One-Time Password (TOTP) authentication. TOTP is a form of two-factor authentication that adds an extra layer of security by requiring users to enter a unique one-time password generated by a TOTP token in addition to their regular login credentials.


### Goal #9 - Audit Trails and Logging


Dengage Solution Capabilities: With extensive audit trails, users can monitor all activities within the platform, including user interactions, campaign launches, and data access. This allows administrators to track any suspicious behavior or unauthorized access. Our logging capabilities provide detailed records of all actions taken within the platform, including user logins, changes to account settings, and campaign modifications. These logs are securely stored and can be easily accessed for review or analysis.
By offering comprehensive audit trails and logging, we help our users maintain compliance with industry regulations and internal security policies.

## DENGAGE RESPONSE TO BUSINESS CONTINUITY


### Goal #1 - Incident Response Plan


Dengage Solution Capabilities: We recognize the paramount importance of business continuity, particularly in the financial sector, and have meticulously crafted our Incident Response Plan (IRP) to meet stringent industry standards and adhere to best practices.
Overview of Dengage's Incident Response Plan:
- Preparation and Prevention: Dengage takes a proactive stance toward potential incidents, employing robust security measures such as encryption protocols, access controls, and regular vulnerability assessments. We continuously update and review our IRP to address emerging threats and ensure preparedness for various scenarios.
- Detection and Analysis: Our platform integrates advanced monitoring tools and anomaly detection systems to swiftly identify any irregular activities or potential security breaches. In the event of an incident, our dedicated team of experts promptly investigates to ascertain the nature and scope of the issue.
- Response and Mitigation: Dengage follows a structured response protocol designed to minimize downtime and mitigate the impact of any disruptions. Our response team is trained to execute predefined procedures swiftly, including isolating affected systems, deploying patches or fixes, and communicating transparently and effectively with stakeholders.
- Recovery and Restoration: We prioritize rapid recovery to restore normal operations without compromising data integrity or service quality. Dengage leverages redundant infrastructure and backup systems to ensure data availability and minimize data loss in the event of an incident.
- Continuous Improvement: Following incidents, we conduct thorough post-mortem analyses to identify lessons learned and areas for improvement. Feedback from incidents informs our ongoing efforts to enhance our systems, processes, and training programs.
Key Features of Dengage's Incident Response Plan:
- 24/7 Monitoring and Support: Our team is available round-the-clock to monitor system health and respond to incidents promptly.
- Regular Drills and Simulation Exercises: We conduct routine drills and simulations to test the effectiveness of our IRP and ensure staff readiness.
- Comprehensive Documentation: Our IRP documentation is regularly updated and readily accessible to all relevant stakeholders, facilitating swift and coordinated response efforts.
- Collaboration with Third-Party Experts: Dengage collaborates with industry-leading security firms and experts to stay abreast of emerging threats and leverage their expertise in incident response efforts.
In conclusion, Dengage is steadfast in ensuring the highest standards of business continuity and security for your banking institution. Our Incident Response Plan exemplifies this commitment, providing a robust framework to address and mitigate potential disruptions effectively.



### Goal #1 - Data Backups and Disaster Recovery


Dengage Solution Capabilities: Regarding our data backups and disaster recovery plan, Dengage has implemented a comprehensive strategy to safeguard your data and ensure uninterrupted service. Our approach includes the following key components:
- Regular Data Backups: We perform regular backups of all customer data, including email lists, campaigns, and user information. These backups are stored securely in multiple locations to minimize the risk of data loss.
- Redundant Infrastructure: Our email marketing platform is hosted on redundant infrastructure with built-in failover mechanisms. This means that in the event of hardware failure or other technical issues, our systems can automatically switch to backup resources to maintain service availability.
- Disaster Recovery Procedures: In the unlikely event of a major disaster or outage, Dengage has established comprehensive procedures to quickly restore service and minimize downtime. These procedures include rapid deployment of backup systems, communication with customers about the situation, and ongoing monitoring and testing to ensure the effectiveness of our recovery efforts.
- Security Measures: We employ robust security measures to protect your data from unauthorized access, including encryption, access controls, and regular security audits and updates.
- Continuous Improvement: Dengage is committed to continuously improving our data backup and disaster recovery capabilities. We regularly review and update our procedures in response to changes in technology, best practices, and regulatory requirements.

### Dengageâ€™s Risk Management Approach

Qualitative Risk Assessmentâ€”The process of actually assessing the risks against the project assessment criteria.

## ANNEXURES


### Annex 1 (Standard GDPR Compliance)


- Introduction to the GDPR
Consumer data protection and privacy have always been important concerns for organizations that process personal customer data. Responding to the increased incidence of data breaches worldwide, governments are also focusing on consumer data security in the form of new legislation. Organizations are required to comply with these continuously evolving regulations while staying innovative and developing their products and services.
The General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) is European Union legislation that represents the most significant change to data protection laws in the EU since 1995. The GDPR replaces that yearâ€™s Data Protection Directive (Directive 95/46/EC) and unites data protection regulations across the entire EU.
The GDPR presents significant challenges for any organization doing business in Europe or with EU citizens, regardless of where the organization is located. The GDPR specifies non-compliance fines at 4% of a companyâ€™s annual global revenue or â‚¬20 million, whichever is greater. As no organization wants to face huge fines or a negative impact on their reputation among customers, investors, and employees, compliance with the GDPR has received much attention from organizations of all sizes.
Your customers need to know that they can trust you with their data, and trust that you are taking the necessary measures to ensure the security of this information. If an organization fails to comply with the GDPR, it may indicate to the market that the organization is not doing everything it must to keep its customersâ€™ data secure. When this level of trust is missing, it can lead to the loss of customers and employees, drops in share price, and other hazards of a tarnished reputation.
- How the GDPR Applies to DÂ·engage
The GDPR defines two main entities: â€œdata processorâ€ and â€œdata controller.â€ The definitions of relationships between and responsibilities of data controllers and data processors are specified to protect the rights of EU citizens whose personal data is stored or processed. The GDPR describes the particular types of security, IT, and administrative activities that might be considered necessary for handling personal data, including encryption, documentation, and taking measures to ensure the integrity, confidentiality, availability, resilience, assessment, and post-incident recovery of processing systems and services.

**In our business relationship with you, DÂ·engage acts as a data processor, while your company is a data controller.**

Data controller (You) â€“ Controllers bear the primary responsibility for GDPR compliance. As a data controller, you manage customer data, access controls, and authentication.
Data processor (DÂ·engage) â€“ Any entity that processes personal data under the controllerâ€™s instructions. As a data processor, we provide cloud-based software, services, infrastructure, and support. As a data processor, we are obliged to make our best efforts to protect your customer data and enable you to fully meet GDPR requirements.

- Prepared for the GDPR
At DÂ·engage, we are committed to full compliance with everything required by the GDPR, as well as taking additional precautions to ensure the complete security and privacy of your customersâ€™ data. DÂ·engage has always focused on data protection and regulatory compliance, and we are constantly looking for better ways to secure data in transit, at rest, and in use. We strive to meet the demands of our client base of 100+ companies from more than 25 countries to comply with all relevant regional laws and regulations.
We have prepared for GDPR by actively enhancing our products to ensure that your organization meets the data privacy, protection, confidentiality, integrity, and availability challenges that the GDPR places on the data that we handle for your organization.
These preparations have included low-level mapping of PII, data privacy vision and strategy, data protection impact assessments, employee awareness and training, data breach management planning, and others.
- How DÂ·engage Complies with the GDPR
The following table presents the specific ways in which DÂ·engage complies with the requirements of the GDPR.


- Frequently Asked Questions about DÂ·engage and the GDPR

**Is DÂ·engage GDPR compliant?**

Yes, as explained above in this document.

**Is DÂ·engage a Data Processor or a Data Controller?**

As regards your customer data, DÂ·engage is a data processor, while you are a data controller. As your data processor, we are obliged to make our best efforts to protect your customer data and enable you to fully meet the relevant GDPR requirements.

**Does DÂ·engage have a data center in Europe?**

Yes, DÂ·engage operates a data center in Frankfurt, Germany.
DÂ·engage will not transfer the storage or processing of your customersâ€™ data outside the country where it is hosted, in conformance with your instructions as the data controller.

**What data am I allowed to collect from EU citizens and what data am I not allowed to collect?**

The GDPR does not limit the types of personal data that can be collected. Instead, the GDPR requires organizations that collect such data to demonstrate â€œData Security by Designâ€ â€“ a dedicated policy that evidences sound principles of security designed to protect personal data from unauthorized access, use, or other types of processing.

| GDPR Requirement | How DÂ·engage Complies or Enables Your Compliance |
| --- | --- |
| Information and Transparency | Each DÂ·engage instance is configured and documented during the onboarding process. Our onboarding team will describe the features and processes used to store, process, and protect customer data. |
| Access to Data | All personal data for a data subject is available for reporting through the contact profile card and interactive reports, as well as various functions of the DÂ·engage API. |
| Data Portability | All personal customer data in DÂ·engage is available for reporting, export/download, and access via functions of the DÂ·engage API. |
| Privacy by Design | Privacy by design is inherent in all DÂ·engage products, as data protection and privacy have always been an important element in our products and services. While DÂ·engageâ€™s software and systems have been developed based on data protection principles and best practices, you are still responsible for configuring and using them appropriately. |
| Privacy by Default | DÂ·engage products meet the privacy-by-default requirement of GDPR, namely that technical and organizational measures are in place to ensure that, by default, only personal data necessary for a specific purpose is processed. |
| Cross-Border Data Transfer | You can decide which data center location your organization will use for hosting your DÂ·engage instance. Accounts in the European Union are hosted in a data center located in Frankfurt, Germany. DÂ·engage does not transfer your customer data out of the county in which it is hosted. |
| Mechanisms in place to Protect the Data from Loss, Misuse, or Disclosure | For data loss, we implement Data Replica & Backup policies. For misuse and disclosure, we have access monitoring, data logging, and session video recording for access to critical systems. We implement ISO27001 practices and have a valid certification. |
| Erasure of Data | Data purge (in the sense of complete and irrevocable deletion or erasure of an individual customerâ€™s data) is possible by submitting a request to DÂ·engage customer support. Purging of customer data must be explicitly indicated by you for specific customers by specifying each customerâ€™s ID. It may take up to 72 hours from the original erasure request until all data related to a particular customer is completely removed from all DÂ·engage systems.  Deleting a customerâ€™s data will erase all personally identifiable data from DÂ·engage (both current and historical data). However, to maintain the integrity of campaign and application usage analytics, aggregated anonymous data will not be amended when a customerâ€™s data is deleted (for example, a campaignâ€™s â€œincrease inâ€ metric value will not be recalculated once a customerâ€™s data is deleted). This is not a problem vis-aÌ€-vis the GDPR because this aggregated data cannot be connected back to an individual.  You are obliged to make sure that no data from any such customers is delivered to DÂ·engage in the future (e.g., as part of the DÂ·engage Daily Process). |

using middleware such as JSON schema validators or ORM constraints. All user-

parameterized queries and input binding. Code audits and CI/CD pipelines include

requests. JavaScript linting ensures no eval usage, and cookies are marked with

Enforce zero-trust access model Yes source. All users and services must authenticate and are authorized based on defined

background jobs are scoped to perform only necessary functions.

local legal interpretation, and we are open to auditing requirements jointly with the

All password hashes include a randomly generated salt of at least 32 bits, stored
Minimum 32-bit salt for hashing Yes securely and never reused. This prevents rainbow table and dictionary-based attacks
and complies with OWASP recommendations.

|  | Network-level interaction details (IP, ports, scheme) | No | Detailed network-level information (e.g., port numbers, internal service protocols) is not disclosed publicly due to security policies. This information can be shared privately during InfoSec review post shortlisting. Typical architecture uses HTTPS, TLS, and mutual-auth APIs between components. |
|  | Corrective actions post pentest by Kar-Tel or share internal pentest results | Yes | Dengage supports third-party security assessments and penetration testing by client- appointed auditors. Any confirmed vulnerabilities are prioritized based on severity and addressed with a mitigation plan, including patch deployment, WAF configuration, or code refactoring. Change logs and evidence of remediation are shared for compliance signoff. |
| IS - Data Validation | Validate all frontend and backend input data | Yes | All input fields in the frontend UI undergo validation for data type, length, and sanitization using secure JavaScript libraries. Backend APIs enforce schema validation using middleware such as JSON schema validators or ORM constraints. All user- supplied input is sanitized to protect against injection attacks. |
|  | No string concatenation in queries; use ORM best practices | Yes | Dengage follows ORM-based development patterns (e.g., Sequelize, Hibernate) and strictly avoids string-based query concatenation. All database interactions use parameterized queries and input binding. Code audits and CI/CD pipelines include static analysis to detect insecure query constructs. |
|  | Avoid unsafe deserialization | Yes | Deserialization in Dengage is restricted to safe, stateless formats such as JSON and form-encoded payloads. Arbitrary binary or object deserialization is not used. All inbound payloads are parsed using secure parsers with whitelisted schemas, and application logic avoids reflective class loading or dynamic evaluation. |
|  | File validation using magic bytes, secure storage | Yes | Uploaded files (e.g., CSVs) are scanned for valid MIME types and magic bytes before processing. Files are stored in encrypted storage with limited access via signed URLs. Antivirus scans are run at the edge, and upload size limits and content validation rules are enforced. |
|  | Protect frontend against XSS/CSRF, enforce safe output rendering | Yes | Frontend components escape dynamic content, restrict innerHTML use, and validate DOM insertion. CSRF tokens are issued per session and validated on all state-changing requests. JavaScript linting ensures no eval usage, and cookies are marked with HttpOnly, Secure, and SameSite flags. |
|  | Set up CSP, HSTS, and CORS securely | Yes | Dengage enforces a restrictive Content Security Policy, disables inline scripts, and uses strict domain whitelisting. HSTS is enabled on all endpoints. CORS settings limit origin access based on environment and disallow credentialed cross-origin requests unless explicitly required. |
| IS - Authentication | 8+ char passwords with upper/lower case, digit, symbol | Yes | Password policies enforce a minimum length of 8 characters and require a combination of uppercase, lowercase, numeric, and special characters. Passwords expire every 90 days, and reuse of the last 3 passwords is blocked. |
|  | Two-factor authentication via app or SMS | Partial | Authenticator app-based 2FA is supported for admin panel access (TOTP-compliant). SMS-based 2FA is not available by default but can be configured through a third-party SMS gateway integration or custom middleware. We recommend app-based 2FA for its reliability and security. |
|  | Lock + IP block after 5 failed logins; CAPTCHA thereafter | Partial | Dengage supports account lockout after configurable failed login attempts (e.g., 5 tries). CAPTCHA support is available for user-facing forms but not enabled by default for internal logins. IP blocklists are enforced at the WAF/firewall level, which can be configured per client. |
|  | Temporary password change enforced on first login | Yes | Temporary credentials trigger a mandatory password reset on first login. Users cannot access the platform until a permanent password is set. This process is logged and session is invalidated after a timeout if no update occurs. |
|  | Admins to use strong passwords and unique usernames | Yes | Admin account creation enforces unique usernames (email-based) and complex password policies. Passwords must meet strength criteria and are stored using bcrypt hashing. Admin logins are logged with IP and timestamp, and password reuse is restricted. |
|  | Authenticate system components with least privilege | Yes | Each microservice and internal component is assigned dedicated API credentials or IAM roles with limited scopes. No shared secrets or blanket access is permitted. Component-level communication is scoped using firewalls, mutual TLS, and per- service access tokens. |
|  | Enforce zero-trust access model | Yes | Dengage follows a zero-trust approach by validating every access request regardless of source. All users and services must authenticate and are authorized based on defined scopes and roles. Trust is never assumed from internal IPs alone. |
|  | IDOR prevention; per-object access validation and logging | Yes | Every API request that references user-owned resources (e.g., campaign IDs, segment IDs) checks for ownership via session-bound user ID. Unauthorized access attempts are blocked and logged. Role validation is enforced before object-level access. |
| IS - Session Management | Session expiration policies | Yes | Sessions expire after 15 minutes of inactivity by default and require re-authentication. Tokens are rotated and securely invalidated on logout or timeout. Sessions are tied to device fingerprints for added security. |
|  | Least privilege enforcement on all layers | Yes | From database access to UI module visibility, Dengage applies the principle of least privilege. Role-based access defines what each user can view or modify. APIs and background jobs are scoped to perform only necessary functions. |
|  | Per-object permission checks | Yes | Permission checks occur at the object level for all critical operations such as editing a campaign, modifying a segment, or deleting user data. Backend logic validates user identity and role for each operation. |
| IS - Cryptography | Compliance with RK ACS 1073-2007, NIST SP 800-57 | Partial | Dengage aligns with NIST SP 800-57 for key management and cryptographic controls. While we follow global standards, explicit compliance with RK ACS 1073-2007 requires local legal interpretation, and we are open to auditing requirements jointly with the client's security team. |
|  | Use strong encryption (bcrypt, PBKDF2, AES); avoid MD5, DES | Yes | Passwords are hashed using bcrypt with per-user salts. Data at rest is encrypted using AES-256, and all data in transit (APIs, SDK communication) is protected with TLS 1.2+. S3 and RDS instances use encryption at rest enabled by default. |
|  | Minimum 32-bit salt for hashing | Yes | All password hashes include a randomly generated salt of at least 32 bits, stored securely and never reused. This prevents rainbow table and dictionary-based attacks and complies with OWASP recommendations. |
|  | Encrypt all personal data in databases | Partial | Dengage encrypts sensitive data fields such as email, phone number, and device IDs using AES-256 where required. However, not all fields are encrypted by default due to real-time processing needs. Clients can request field-level encryption for additional attributes during onboarding. |

## Page 5

1. Executive Summary
KElectric (KE) seeks a Campaign Management Tool to power its Recovery as a System
(RAAS) initiative enabling data-driven customer segmentation, automated dunning/collections
communications, and omnichannel customer engagement. DÂ·engage is pleased to present a
comprehensive proposal addressing these needs. Our Customer Experience Data Platform
(CXDP) offers a unified, cloud-first solution (with on-premise flexibility) that will integrate
seamlessly with KEs systems and modernize recovery processes. We propose to deliver an end-
to-end platform that unifies customer data, orchestrates personalized campaigns across Email,
SMS, WhatsApp, mobile app, IVR and more, and provides real-time analytics and AI-driven
insights to maximize recovery outcomes.
In this response, we first introduce DÂ·engages company background and MarTech leadership.
We then detail the Technical Proposal covering our platform architecture, data integration
approach, AI led omnichannel orchestration capabilities, advanced AI segmentation (including
RFM and AI/ML personalization via our Zeki AI engine), journey automation, integration with KEs
SAP and other systems, geo-intelligent disconnection workflows, performance analytics, security,
and scalability features. We include a Functional Compliance Matrix mapping each of KEs
requirements to DÂ·engages capabilities, ensuring a point-by-point alignment. Next, our
Implementation Methodology outlines a phased deployment starting from proof-of-concept
through pilot, full rollout, user training, and change management with clear timelines and
performance benchmarks. We also attached a few annexures covering Service Level Agreement
(SLA) commitments, Case Studies of similar successes: MCB Funds, Fibabanka, and Burgan
Bank, Certifications, Detailed Escalation Matrices, Security Guidelines, as per KEs requirements.
DÂ·engages proposal is tailored to KEs utility context, emphasizing no-code usability for business
teams and robust enterprise security for IT compliance. Our solution will empower KE to execute
intelligent, automated recovery campaigns that reach the right customer at the right time via the
best channel, with minimal manual effort, thus improving collection rates and customer
experience. We are confident that DÂ·engages proven CXDP platform and our expertise in large-
scale implementations will help KE realize the RAAS vision and set a new benchmark for data-
driven revenue recovery in the utility sector.
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 5

DÂ·engages Customer Experience Data Platform (CXDP) is designed with deployment flexibility
at its core. While our architecture is inherently cloud-firstoptimized for scalability,
uptime, and fast iterationwe fully support on-premises and hybrid models when needed.
This means KE can choose the model that best aligns with its IT strategy, regulatory needs, and
integration ecosystem. Regardless of the deployment mode, DÂ·engage ensures high resilience,
consistent performance, and audit-grade availability for RAAS-critical campaigns.

Kubernetes orchestration for performance and reliability.

data center (on-prem), or via a hybrid approach.

RFP, ensuring data sovereignty and seamless integration with SAP, GIS, or other internal
systems.

infrastructure ensure continuous campaign operations.

downtime even at multi-million daily scale [Case Study Attached]

2.6 Campaign Performance Tracking, Dashboards, and Predictive Analytics
Measuring outcomes and learning from every interaction is essential to the RAAS strategy.
DÂ·engage includes a powerful real-time analytics and reporting suite, enabling KE to track
campaign performance, customer behavior, and business outcomes all from within the
platform. From cost-benefit analysis to predictive dashboards, every layer of insight is covered.
Real-time dashboards for each campaign or journey show delivery rates, open/click
rates, conversions (e.g., payments), and recovery amounts
Build custom reports on campaign costs vs recovery outcomes
Prebuilt templates: Campaign Performance Summary, Channel Performance by Day,
Segment Response Report
Every message sent and response logged centrally per customer
Powers KEs centralized communication log and audit trail for RAAS
Track engagement by channel (e.g., SMS vs WhatsApp) and by content variant
Run A/B tests to determine what message or timing works best
Use funnel, cohort, and retention analysis to learn what works overtime
Integrate KEs predictive models or use DÂ·engages built-in AI scores for forecasting
recovery rates
Include likelihood-to-pay metrics in dashboards for management decision-making
Link engagement metrics to actual payments when payment data is connected
Attribution models show which campaigns led to what revenue
Supports control group comparisons (uplift vs baseline) for better RAAS strategy validation

5. KE Use Case Portfolio Powered by DÂ·engage CXDP
K-Electrics customer base is vast, diverse, and highly dynamic spanning households,
industries, and commercial hubs. While the RAAS initiative focuses on optimizing recoveries, the
same DÂ·engage platform that powers RAAS can become KEs central engine for all customer
engagement, across every operational domain. Below is a set of select few practical, high-impact
scenarios tailored for a utility environment like KEs, blending immediate RAAS value with long-
term customer experience benefits.
1. Intelligent Recovery Journeys (RAAS-focused)
Scenario: A high-risk defaulter segment is flagged by KEs analytics module.
How DÂ·engage Works: The platform instantly triggers a multi-step journey SMS on day one,
WhatsApp follow-up on day three, and an automated IVR call on day seven escalating only if
no payment is detected. If the customer pays midway, the system halts further reminders and
sends a thank-you message.
Impact: Shortens recovery cycles, reduces cost per recovery, and ensures a respectful, data-
driven approach.
2. Personalized Payment Reminder Cadence (RAAS-focused)
Scenario: KE notices customers have different payment habits some pay early, others at the
last minute.
How DÂ·engage Works: Historical payment behavior feeds into the segmentation engine. Early
payers get a gentle heads-up well before due date; late payers get last-minute reminders through
their most responsive channel.
Impact: Maximizes on-time payments while reducing unnecessary message volume.
3. Targeted Energy Conservation Campaigns (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: Summer peak load approaches, and KE wants to encourage reduced consumption
during critical hours.
How DÂ·engage Works: Using usage pattern data, the system identifies high-consumption
households during peak slots and sends them tailored conservation tips, incentives, and progress
tracking.
Impact: Reduces grid strain and positions KE as a partner in sustainable living.
4. Proactive Outage Communication (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: A feeder line in Clifton will undergo maintenance tomorrow from 9 AM to 2 PM.
How DÂ·engage Works: Affected customers are geo-targeted and sent advance notice via SMS,
app notifications, and email. On the day, a maintenance started alert goes out, followed by
service restored confirmation.
Impact: Reduces inbound complaints and builds trust by keeping customers informed.
5. High-Usage Alerts (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: A customers usage is suddenly 40% higher than their monthly average.
How DÂ·engage Works: The anomaly is detected and triggers a friendly alert, suggesting possible
causes (e.g., appliance fault, seasonal change) along with conservation tips.
Impact: Prevents bill shock and demonstrates KEs proactive service stance.
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 20

6. Predictive Maintenance Notifications (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: Meter diagnostics indicate a potential fault developing.
How DÂ·engage Works: Customers receive automated messages prompting them to schedule a

Impact: Minimizes downtime and costly emergency repairs.
7. New Customer Onboarding Journeys (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: A customer gets a new connection approved.
How DÂ·engage Works: Over the next 14 days, they receive a welcome email with account setup
instructions, a WhatsApp message with their first bill explainer, and a mobile push with energy-
saving tips.
Impact: Faster adoption of self-service tools, reduced first-bill confusion, and better early
engagement.
8. Geo-Targeted Emergency Alerts (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: A sudden monsoon storm causes localized flooding in Saddar.
How DÂ·engage Works: Customers in the affected grid are alerted with safety guidelines, outage
information, and updates on restoration progress.
Impact: Enhances public safety and improves KEs crisis response reputation.
9. Customer Loyalty & Engagement Programs (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: KE wants to reward consistent payers and eco-conscious customers.
How DÂ·engage Works: Segmentation identifies high-engagement customers who then receive
exclusive offers such as bill discounts, early access to new services, or invitations to KE
community events.
Impact: Strengthens long-term relationships and encourages positive behaviors.
10. Automated Customer Feedback Loops (Non-RAAS & RAAS)
Scenario: A payment is made, a complaint is resolved, or a disconnection is avoided.
How DÂ·engage Works: The platform sends a short survey immediately after the event, capturing
satisfaction scores and comments. Feedback is analyzed to detect recurring service issues.
Impact: Turns every interaction into a learning opportunity for operational improvement.
11. Educational Communication (Non-RAAS)
Scenario: Proactive Energy Conservation, know-your-bill Education
How DÂ·engage Works: Uses KEs consumption data to send personalized, seasonal energy-
saving tips via SMS, email, or app notifications. Messages are tailored to each customers usage
patterns, climate conditions, and appliance behavior.
Impact: Builds long-term conservation habits and strengthens KEs role as a trusted energy
advisor.
In essence, these scenarios demonstrate how DÂ·engage can move KE from reactive, siloed
communication to a proactive, personalized, and omnichannel engagement model. Whether its
recovering overdue payments, preventing outages, educating customers, or building loyalty,
DÂ·engage becomes the single engagement brain optimizing every customer touchpoint.

6. Platform Detailed Overview
6.1 DÂ·engage Customer Experience Data Platform (CXDP)
DÂ·engage combines a flexible Customer Data Platform with marketing automation, enabling
marketers to activate data, run journeys, and drive resultswithout relying on IT. It connects
data, channels, and intelligence in a single platform.
What the CXDP enables:
Unify customer data: Break down silos and build real-time customer profiles by ingesting
behavioral, transactional, CRM, offline, and third-party data. A low-code, schema-flexible
setup ensures fast onboarding and easy alignment with your data model.
Segment and activate audiences: Create dynamic segments using behaviors, attributes,
or predictive scores. Segments auto-update based on live data, ensuring relevance in
every interaction.
Orchestrate cross-channel engagement: Automate journeys across email, SMS, push,
in-app, WhatsApp, and more. Flows adapt in real time to user behavior and lifecycle
triggersall from one interface.
Measure what matters: Analyze performance with deep insightsRFM scoring, funnel
analysis, uplift, and revenue attributionso you know whats working and where to
optimize.
Scale intelligence with AI: Use built-in models for send time, channel affinity, churn or
purchase likelihood, and predictive CLV. Focus effort and spend where it drives the most
impactwithout manual guesswork.

6.20 App Uninstall Tracking
DÂ·engages Uninstall tracking module helps marketers understand whats driving user churn
beyond standard campaign metrics. By analyzing uninstall behavior across devices and
platforms, you gain visibility into post-campaign fallout and can proactively optimize retention.
Key capabilities:
Behavioral Insights: Identify which campaigns lead to app uninstalls, helping you refine
your messaging and avoid negative impact.
Churn Prediction: Use uninstall likelihood scoring (low/medium/high) to anticipate risk
and take preventive action.
Audience Breakdown: Track uninstalls by device model, app version, and platform
(iOS, Android, Huawei) for more granular analysis.
Push Attribution: See which push campaigns contribute to uninstalls and adjust
strategies accordingly.
Reachability Intelligence: Understand how many uninstalled users were also reachable
via Email, SMS, or Web Push.
Silent Push Support: Run background notifications without disrupting the user
experience, helping to maintain engagement quietly.
By connecting uninstall behavior to campaign activity, marketers can detect early warning signs
and make smarter retention decisions before its too late.

6.24 AI Powered Predictive User Behavior
DÂ·engages predictive behavior filtersLikelihood to Pay, Likelihood to Churn, and
Likelihood to Uninstalluse machine learning to anticipate future user actions. These insights
allow marketers to act preemptively, tailoring their messaging and strategy before users
disengage, uninstall, or drop out of the funnel.
Likelihood to Pay predicts the probability of a user making a payment event within a defined
time window.
Launch Targeted Campaigns to high-intent users with tailored offers and urgency-based
CTAs
Drive Smarter Recommendations by aligning content and products with buying
propensity
Improve Demand Forecasting by linking predictive intent to inventory planning
Likelihood to Churn identifies users who are at risk of becoming inactive or leaving.
Deploy Retention Campaigns before churn happens, using incentives or re-engagement
journeys
Uncover Experience Gaps by analyzing behavioral patterns behind churn signals
Prioritize High-Value Users for proactive support or loyalty rewards
Likelihood to Uninstall detects users most likely to uninstall your mobile app.
Use cases include:
Fixing Drop-Off Points in the onboarding flow or user journey

that can be redeemed for real-world rewards.
Leaderboards: Fostering competition and social interaction by ranking users based on
their performance.
Progress bars and levels: Visualizing user progress and unlocking new features or
rewards as they advance.
Personalized experiences: Tailoring gamification elements to individual user preferences
and behavior.

## Page 52

7.2 Change Management
At DÂ·engage, the Change Management Process governs the controlled introduction of changes
to the customer engagement platform and related project environments. It ensures that changes
are clearly defined, properly evaluated, appropriately authorized, and implemented with minimal
disruption to ongoing operations or project objectives.
This process is central to maintaining high system availability, delivery timelines, campaign
integrity, and stakeholder trust. Poorly managed changes can lead to operational downtime,
regression issues, or deviation from agreed success metrics. Therefore, DÂ·engage enforces a
rigorous, audit-ready change management policy where all changes are tracked from
request through implementation to closure.
The primary objective of the DÂ·engage Change Management Process is to:
Minimize service disruption or risk to business operations
Align technical changes with RAAS project priorities and SLAs
Ensure all stakeholders (KE business, IT, field ops, and vendor teams) remain informed
and coordinated
Maintain traceability, rollback plans, and compliance logs
DÂ·engage implements five structured stages for every platform change, configuration update,
or integration adjustment introduced within the RAAS Campaign Management environment:
1. Submission & Acknowledgement of Change Requests
o Change Requests (CRs) are submitted via ticketing system.
o Each CR includes justification, risk categorization, intended scope, and requestor
details.
o A unique Change ID is assigned, and formal acknowledgment is sent within 1
business day.
2. Review & Logging
o A cross-functional Change Advisory Board (CAB) logs the CR and conducts an
initial screening.
o Classification into Minor, Standard, Major, or Emergency Change based on impact
and urgency.
o Change is recorded in the Change Management Register with version control.
3. Impact & Feasibility Assessment
o Technical, business, security, and SLA impacts are evaluated by relevant platform
owners.
o Dependencies, rollback plans, and test protocols are documented.
o Risk scoring is applied using a predefined matrix (e.g., customer impact, data
integrity, compliance).
4. Approval & Scheduling
o CAB or designated authority (based on change type) grants formal approval.
o For high-risk or cross-system changes, client-side stakeholders (e.g., KE IT Ops)
are looped in for joint validation.
o An implementation schedule is finalized in coordination with campaign blackout
windows or system freeze periods.
5. Implementation, Validation & Closure
o Changes are deployed in lower DEV environments and tested before production
rollout.
o Post-deployment validation includes functional testing, system monitoring, and
business sign-off.
o Upon successful implementation, change is closed with updated logs, learnings,
and rollback plan archive.
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 52

## Page 53

Policy Commitments
All production changes require pre-approval.
Emergency changes must be documented within 24 hours post-deployment.
Each change record includes audit trail of who approved, who deployed, and post-change
outcomes.
Weekly or bi-weekly CAB reviews are held for active and backlog change requests.
Risks of Non-Compliance
Any deviation from the Change Management Process introduces:
Risk of unplanned service interruptions or customer impact
Violation of agreed SLAs or business continuity standards
Gaps in system integrity and rollback preparedness
Reduced visibility and control for stakeholders
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 53

## Page 54

7.3 Risk & Issue Management
The DÂ·engage Risk & Issue Management framework establishes a structured approach to
proactively identify, assess, mitigate, and monitor risks and issues that could affect the
performance, delivery, or integrity of the RAAS Campaign Management Tool. It ensures visibility,
traceability, and accountability across all phases of the engagement from deployment to
ongoing operations.
DÂ·engage adheres to a preventive-first principle detecting risks early, applying impact-limiting
actions, and ensuring escalation protocols are clearly defined. This is critical in complex
environments like K-Electrics, where campaign performance, regulatory compliance, and
customer experience must be continuously safeguarded.
The goal of this framework is to:
Proactively anticipate potential disruptions (risks) and respond decisively to actual
problems (issues)
Enable a consistent, transparent way for all project and operations teams to record and
address challenges
Maintain RAAS program continuity, uptime, and effectiveness by minimizing unresolved
risks
Align stakeholders around mitigation strategies, contingency planning, and responsibility
matrices
DÂ·engage applies a lifecycle-based approach across five steps:
Identification & Logging
Any stakeholder (internal or client-side) can raise a risk or issue.
Entries are logged in the centralized Risk & Issue Register, tagged as:
o Risk: A potential future event with uncertainty
o Issue: A current, active problem impacting delivery or performance
Assessment & Categorization
Each item is evaluated on:
o Impact (Low / Medium / High / Critical)
o Likelihood (Rare / Unlikely / Likely / Almost Certain)
Risks are categorized (e.g., technical, operational, data, campaign, compliance).
Issues are marked with severity, affected modules, and urgency of resolution.
Mitigation / Resolution Planning
Risks receive mitigation plans, contingency strategies, and owners.
Issues receive action plans, timelines, and interim measures (e.g., workarounds).
Owners are assigned based on function (e.g., Campaign Ops, Data Integration, Client
Success).
Monitoring & Escalation
All open items are reviewed weekly (or more frequently during critical phases).
Escalation matrix defines who gets notified at which severity level (KE vs DÂ·engage vs
third parties).
Status is updated continuously: Open In Progress Mitigated / Resolved / Deferred
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 54

## Page 55

Closure & Retrospective
Items are formally closed only after validation and documentation.
Lessons learned from critical risks/issues are reviewed quarterly and built into platform
playbooks, automations, or alerts.
Policy Highlights
Each risk/issue must have an assigned owner, target date, and priority level
The register is maintained in real-time and shared weekly with KE stakeholders
High-severity issues are escalated within 2 business hours
Critical risks have mitigation plans pre-reviewed by the DÂ·engage Program Manager
Reporting & Governance
Summary dashboards provided to KE PMO bi-weekly
Status codes: Open, Mitigated, Accepted Risk, Deferred, Resolved
Option to integrate with KEs internal PMIS/Jira for real-time sync
Risk Control Culture at DÂ·engage
We maintain a no surprise policy early flagging is encouraged
All project staff are trained on platform risks and escalation protocols
We conduct monthly risk audits to ensure coverage and accountability
Karachi Electric | RAAS Project 1100033779 Campaign Management Tool 55
